## Families

- **Room to connect** (`connect`): free grid capacity, hosting capacity or connection queues per substation, node or area (Grid capacity layer, site analysis). have 6, built 21, not published 14, blocked 0.
- **Every generating unit** (`units`): national registers of generating units (layer per country under Power plants). have 2, built 8, not published 19, blocked 0.
- **Live mix and CO₂** (`live_mix`): generation by source at the operator's own time step, and CO₂ per kWh where the operator publishes it (country cards). have 23, built 4, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **Redispatch and curtailment** (`congestion`): redispatch, countertrading, congestion-management costs and curtailment (country cards, agent catalogue). have 0, built 15, not published 14, blocked 0.
- **Wholesale prices** (`prices`): day-ahead and imbalance (or real-time) prices (Prices views, imbalance card). have 24, built 3, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **What consumers pay** (`retail`): household and business electricity and gas prices (retail colouring, country report). have 28, built 1, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Emitters per installation** (`emitters`): verified CO₂ per installation (CO₂ per installation layer). have 28, built 1, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Plants offline now** (`offline`): generating units out of service now (planned and forced). have 26, built 1, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **EV charging** (`ev`): public charge points (EV charging layer) and counts per country. have 1, built 28, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Gas** (`gas`): gas flows, storage, LNG and prices. have 26, built 1, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **Reliability** (`reliability`): interruptions per customer (SAIDI, SAIFI) or outages per area. have 1, built 28, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Outlines at every level** (`outlines`): municipality, neighbourhood and small-area outlines for areas, choropleths and resolve_area (brief 27 B1); EU LAU left out (GISCO: non-commercial only). have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **City outlines (GHS urban centres)** (`urban_centres`): GHS Urban Centre Database R2024: city outlines worldwide with their 2025 population. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Rasters summed in any area** (`rasters`): people and built-up surface (GHSL), land cover (WorldCover), slope (Copernicus DEM), solar and wind resource, river flood depth, read by area_stats. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Energy consumption per small area** (`consumption`): electricity and gas use per municipality or smaller area, MWh a year (or averages as published), with the level named. have 0, built 10, not published 19, blocked 0.
- **Heating fuel per small area** (`heating`): how homes are heated per small area, as published (counts or shares). have 0, built 7, not published 21, blocked 1.
- **Energy poverty** (`energy_poverty`): households unable to keep warm, fuel poverty or energy burden, at the finest level published. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Industrial facilities and releases** (`industrial`): every reporting facility with its position and releases to air and water; large combustion plants' fuel input. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Oil and gas facilities** (`oil_gas_facilities`): refineries, processing plants, storage and terminals, pipelines. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **CO₂ storage** (`co2_storage`): geological storage units and their capacity. have 0, built 28, not published 0, blocked 1.
- **Heat demand** (`heat_demand`): heat demand density (100 m) and industrial excess heat sites. have 0, built 28, not published 1, blocked 0.
- **Fires** (`fires`): active fire detections and burnt areas near the grid. have 0, built 1, not published 0, blocked 28.
- **Flood zones** (`flood_zones`): river flood hazard, and national flood zones where published. have 28, built 1, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Wetlands** (`wetlands`): wetlands as siting constraints. have 0, built 0, not published 0, blocked 29.
- **Dams and reservoirs** (`dams`): barriers and reservoirs (Global Dam Watch). have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Who owns the assets** (`ownership`): owners and ownership chains of GEM-tracked plants, mines and pipelines. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Renewables acceleration and zoning areas** (`acceleration_areas`): areas designated for renewables (RED III Art. 15c. have 0, built 1, not published 28, blocked 0.
- **Agricultural land quality** (`farmland_quality`): agricultural land classification or prime farmland. have 0, built 1, not published 28, blocked 0.
- **Brownfield and contaminated land** (`brownfield`): brownfield registers and polluted sites. have 0, built 3, not published 26, blocked 0.

- **Heritage and aviation constraints** (`heritage_aviation`): listed heritage. have 0, built 41, not published 0, blocked 0.

## Cells that are not on the map

- Room to connect · BE · not_published: Elia and Sibelga answer HTTP 403 behind Cloudflare's check (2026-10-27); Elia's open-data portal holds no hosting-capacity set; Fluvius and ORES pages answer 404. Browser discovery in Actions (2026-10-28, an honest Chromium) did not pass the check. Second pass (2026-11-05): Searched Elia Open Data (110 datasets listed): ods183 congestion risk indicator per electrical zone exists but is an operational signal, not connection capacity: be_elia_congestion_risk (left_out). Elia's hosting-capacity map still behind Cloudflare. (https://www.elia.be/en/customers/connection/grid-hosting-capacity, checked 2026-11-05)
- Room to connect · CY · not_published: TSO Cyprus answers HTTP 403 behind Cloudflare; EAC's hosting-capacity page resets the connection (2026-10-27). Browser discovery in Actions (2026-10-28, an honest Chromium) did not pass the check. Second pass (2026-11-06): Searched in Greek and English (EAC DSO site pilot.eac.com.cy WordPress search/media API: 'δυναμικότητα', 'χωρητικότητα', 'hosting capacity'; TSOC and CERA answer 403 behind Cloudflare). - Redispatch and curtailment · PL · built: ENTSO-E publishes cross-border redispatch (A63/A46), congestion costs (A92) for this control area (2025-09 to 2026-10); nothing published for internal redispatch (A63/A85), countertrading (A91) (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Redispatch and curtailment · PT · built: ENTSO-E publishes countertrading (A91), congestion costs (A92) for this control area (2025-09 to 2026-10); nothing published for internal redispatch (A63/A85), cross-border redispatch (A63/A46) (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Redispatch and curtailment · RO · not_published: ENTSO-E answered 'No matching data' for internal and cross-border redispatch, countertrading and congestion costs over 400 days (2025-09-15 to 2026-10-20, every month asked); no national series found as data Second pass (2026-11-06): Searched in Romanian ('redispecerizare', 'congestii', 'contrapartidă', Transelectrica, ANRE): Transelectrica's market, balancing and system-services pages link no redispatch data; congestion costs appear only in quarterly financial reports and the ANRE-approved structural congestion report (PDF, 2020). Not published as data. (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-11-06)
- Redispatch and curtailment · SK · not_published: ENTSO-E answered 'No matching data' for internal and cross-border redispatch, countertrading and congestion costs over 400 days (2025-09-15 to 2026-10-20, every month asked); no national series found as data Second pass (2026-11-05): Searched in Slovak ('redispečing', SEPS, ÚRSO): only tender documents and ÚRSO decisions (PDF), no series as data. (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-11-05)
- Redispatch and curtailment · SI · not_published: ENTSO-E answered 'No matching data' for internal and cross-border redispatch, countertrading and congestion costs over 400 days (2025-09-15 to 2026-10-20, every month asked); no national series found as data Second pass (2026-11-06): Searched in Slovenian ('redispečing', 'prerazporejanje', 'omejitve', ELES, Agencija za energijo, podatki.gov.si: 0 datasets): ELES operation, system-services and notices pages carry no redispatch or countertrading data; only cross-border capacity, balancing auctions and the hosting-capacity PDF (connect). Not published as data. (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-11-06)
- Redispatch and curtailment · ES · built: ENTSO-E publishes countertrading (A91), congestion costs (A92) for this control area (2025-09 to 2026-10); nothing published for internal redispatch (A63/A85), cross-border redispatch (A63/A46) (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Redispatch and curtailment · SE · not_published: ENTSO-E answered 'No matching data' for internal and cross-border redispatch, countertrading and congestion costs over 400 days (2025-09-15 to 2026-10-20, every month asked); no national series found as data Second pass (2026-11-05): Searched SVK's data service (Swedish 'mothandel', 'specialreglering', 'countertrade', 'redispatch'): its CKAN lists 10 packages, none on countertrade or redispatch; Mimer states no licence. Still not published as open data. (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-11-05)
- Redispatch and curtailment · GB · built: published at country level (NESO constraint cost breakdown) (https://api.neso.energy/api/3/action/package_search?q=constraint%20breakdown, checked 2026-11-05)
- Redispatch and curtailment · US · built: California's wind and solar curtailment (5-min, local and system; CAISO's newest file ends December 2025); ERCOT, MISO, SPP, PJM, NYISO and ISO-NE publish no curtailment file found as data (https://www.caiso.com/library/production-curtailments-data, checked 2026-10-20)

- Plants offline now · CY · not_published: ENTSO-E A80 for Cyprus (10YCY-1001A0003J) answered no outage document (wave7 job H, 2026-10-20); TSO Cyprus and EAC publish no list of units offline (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Plants offline now · IE · have: The SEM zone (Ireland and Northern Ireland) is queried since wave 7; it reported no unit offline at the first read (2026-10-20 03:55 UTC), listed as 0 MW (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Plants offline now · LU · have: Luxembourg shares the DE-LU bidding zone; its units (Vianden) report there and are counted under Germany (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Plants offline now · MT · not_published: ENTSO-E A80 for Malta (10Y1001A1001A93C) answered no outage document (wave7 job H, 2026-10-20); Enemalta publishes no list of units offline (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Plants offline now · US · have: US markets publish no unit outages; fossil units at zero output hour by hour (https://campd.epa.gov, checked 2026-10-15)

- Pumped hydro potential · AT · blocked: the site lists come only through the Pumped Hydro Shortlisting Tool, which needs a registered account (Register / Login, "Accounts are also used to manage user permissions"); the map layers are rendered WMS only, and no licence beyond "please acknowledge the RE100 Group" is stated (2026-11-05). Data request: RE100 Group, ANU: the global greenfield and bluefield PHES site lists (coordinates, head, energy GWh, storage hours, cost class) as an open file under a stated licence that allows commercial reuse (https://re100.anu.edu.au/shortlisting/, checked 2026-11-06)

- Night lights · AT · blocked: listing is open (VNP46A4 annual composites 2012-2025; 2025 = 540 HDF5 tiles, 53.1 GB, 77-180 MB each), but every file answers HTTP 303 to /profiles/licenses/... (Earthdata login); NASA_EARTHDATA_TOKEN not set (2026-11-04); CRS, resolution, band and unit not read from a file (no token). Data request: set NASA_EARTHDATA_TOKEN (https://urs.earthdata.nasa.gov, free account) as a repo secret; then fetch only the Europe tiles (https://ladsweb.modaps.eosdis.nasa.gov/archive/allData/5200/VNP46A4/, checked 2026-11-06)
